c:["$","p",null,{"children":["Shadows estimate properties of a few qubits at a time. 35:["$","p",null,{"children":["To reduce the statistical noise of the shadows, the paper uses ",["$","em",null,{"children":"common randomized measurements"}]," (CRM) ",["$","$L2",null,{"href":"#references","children":"[5]"}],". For each setting, it subtracts the average shadow that a prior guess of the state would produce in the same setting, and then adds back the prior's exact value.
